Method: 让模型学习将响应与唯一溯源标签关联，指示责任数据集；基于此能力支持针对性测试时修复，可选择性触发对指定数据源的针对性拒绝，无需重新训练或修改模型参数

Result: 实验表明DebugLM在多阶段训练管道中提供准确的行为溯源，实现有效的测试时修复，同时保持模型的通用效用

Conclusion: DebugLM通过为LLM提供内置数据溯源能力，解决了LLM训练中数据来源可观测性不足的问题，实现了精确的行为追踪和针对性修复

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained through multi-stage pipelines over heterogeneous data sources, yet developers lack a principled way to pinpoint the specific data responsible for an observed behavior. This lack of observability reduces debugging to reactive patching and makes failures prone to recur under distribution shift or subsequent model updates. To address this limitation, we propose DebugLM, a framework that equips LLMs with built-in data provenance, enabling them to explicitly trace the origins of their behaviors to specific training data sources. Specifically, the model learns to associate its responses with unique provenance tags that indicate the responsible dataset, empowering developers to precisely identify where undesirable behaviors are learned. Building on this capability, DebugLM further supports targeted test-time remediation, enabling developers to selectively trigger targeted refusal for specified data sources without retraining or modifying model parameters. Experiments demonstrate that DebugLM provides accurate behavior tracing in multi-stage training pipelines and effective test-time remediation while preserving the general utility of the model.

</details>


### [41] [IndicSafe: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multilingual LLM Safety in South Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17915)
*Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Sanchari Chowdhuri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了12种印度语言中LLM的安全性，揭示了显著的安全漂移现象，并发布了首个印度语言安全基准IndicSafe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多语言环境中部署时，其在文化多样、资源匮乏语言中的安全行为仍不明确。印度语言使用人口超过12亿但在LLM训练数据中代表性不足，需要系统评估其安全性。

Method: 使用包含6,000个文化相关提示的数据集，涵盖种姓、宗教、性别、健康和政治等主题，评估10个主流LLM在12种印度语言翻译变体上的表现。采用提示级熵、类别偏置分数和多语言一致性指数量化失败情况。

Result: 发现显著安全漂移：跨语言一致性仅12.8%，安全率方差超过17%。部分模型在低资源脚本中过度拒绝良性提示，过度标记政治敏感话题，而其他模型则未能标记不安全生成。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM存在关键的安全泛化差距，安全对齐在不同语言间转移不均。发布了首个支持文化知情安全评估的基准IndicSafe，倡导基于区域危害的语言感知对齐策略。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in multilingual settings, their safety behavior in culturally diverse, low-resource languages remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic evaluation of LLM safety across 12 Indic languages, spoken by over 1.2 billion people but underrepresented in LLM training data. Using a dataset of 6,000 culturally grounded prompts spanning caste, religion, gender, health, and politics, we assess 10 leading LLMs on translated variants of the prompt.
  Our analysis reveals significant safety drift: cross-language agreement is just 12.8\%, and \texttt{SAFE} rate variance exceeds 17\% across languages. Some models over-refuse benign prompts in low-resource scripts, overflag politically sensitive topics, while others fail to flag unsafe generations. We quantify these failures using prompt-level entropy, category bias scores, and multilingual consistency indices.
  Our findings highlight critical safety generalization gaps in multilingual LLMs and show that safety alignment does not transfer evenly across languages. We release \textsc{IndicSafe}, the first benchmark to enable culturally informed safety evaluation for Indic deployments, and advocate for language-aware alignment strategies grounded in regional harms.

</details>


### [42] [Efficient Training-Free Multi-Token Prediction via Embedding-Space Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17942)
*Raghavv Goel,Mukul Gagrani,Mingu Lee,Chris Lott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的多令牌预测方法，通过掩码令牌探测LLM实现并行预测，减少模型调用并提升生成吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然仅训练用于下一个令牌预测，但具有潜在的多令牌预测能力。现有方法通常需要修改模型权重或依赖辅助草稿模型，我们希望开发一种简单、无需训练的方法来利用这种能力。

Method: 提出基于掩码令牌探测的方法：1) 从模型嵌入空间中动态生成掩码令牌进行探测；2) 构建推测令牌树，从掩码令牌logits中采样top-K候选；3) 应用轻量级剪枝策略保留高概率延续；4) 在解码时并行验证候选预测，实现无损生成。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法始终优于现有的无需训练基线：在LLaMA3上接受长度增加约12%，在Qwen3上增加8-12%，吞吐量提升达15-19%。理论分析和实证证据表明解码器层自然地将掩码令牌表示与下一个令牌状态对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法成功利用了LLM的潜在多令牌预测能力，无需修改模型权重或依赖辅助模型，实现了高效的并行预测和显著的吞吐量提升，为LLM推理优化提供了新的训练免费解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit latent multi-token prediction (MTP) capabilities despite being trained solely for next-token generation. We propose a simple, training-free MTP approach that probes an LLM using on-the-fly mask tokens drawn from its embedding space, enabling parallel prediction of future tokens without modifying model weights or relying on auxiliary draft models. Our method constructs a speculative token tree by sampling top-K candidates from mask-token logits and applies a lightweight pruning strategy to retain high-probability continuations. During decoding, candidate predictions are verified in parallel, resulting in lossless generation while substantially reducing the number of model calls and improving token throughput. Across benchmarks, our probing-based MTP consistently outperforms existing training-free baselines, increasing acceptance length by approximately 12\% on LLaMA3 and 8--12\% on Qwen3, and achieving throughput gains of up to 15--19\%. Finally, we provide theoretical insights and empirical evidence showing that decoder layers naturally align mask-token representations with next-token states, enabling accurate multi-step prediction without retraining or auxiliary models.

</details>


### [43] [ShapleyLaw: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multilingual Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17945)
*Xuyang Cao,Qianying Liu,Chuan Xiao,Yusuke Oda,Pontus Stenetorp,Daisuke Kawahara,Makoto Onizuka,Sadao Kurohashi,Shuyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ShapleyLaw方法，使用合作博弈论中的Shapley值量化跨语言迁移效应，优化多语言预训练中的语言混合比例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言缩放定律无法准确衡量跨语言迁移效应，导致语言混合比例优化不理想。需要一种能量化各语言贡献的方法来更好地预测模型性能和优化语言配比。

Method: 将多语言预训练建模为合作博弈，每种语言作为玩家共同贡献于预训练过程。使用Shapley值量化每种语言在游戏中的贡献，提出基于博弈论的ShapleyLaw多语言缩放定律。

Result: 实验表明ShapleyLaw在模型性能预测和语言混合比例优化方面优于基线方法，能更准确地反映跨语言迁移效应。

Conclusion: 通过合作博弈论框架量化跨语言迁移效应，提出的ShapleyLaw方法能有效改进多语言预训练中的语言混合比例优化，提升模型性能预测准确性。

Abstract: In multilingual pretraining, the test loss of a pretrained model is heavily influenced by the proportion of each language in the pretraining data, namely the \textit{language mixture ratios}. Multilingual scaling laws can predict the test loss under different language mixture ratios and can therefore be used to estimate the optimal ratios. However, the current approaches to multilingual scaling laws do not measure the \textit{cross-lingual transfer} effect, resulting in suboptimal mixture ratios. In this paper, we consider multilingual pretraining as a cooperative game in which each language acts as a player that jointly contributes to pretraining, gaining the resulting reduction in test loss as the payoff. Consequently, from the perspective of cooperative game theory, we quantify the cross-lingual transfer from each language by its contribution in the game, and propose a game-theoretic multilingual scaling law called \textit{ShapleyLaw}. Our experiments show that ShapleyLaw outperforms baseline methods in model performance prediction and language mixture optimization.

</details>


### [44] [Gender Disambiguation in Machine Translation: Diagnostic Evaluation in Decoder-Only Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17952)
*Chiara Manna,Hosein Mohebbi,Afra Alishahi,Frédéric Blain,Eva Vanmassenhove*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种评估机器翻译中性别偏见的新框架，引入了"先验偏见"概念来衡量模型的默认性别假设，并将该框架应用于仅解码器模型。研究发现仅解码器模型在性别特定指标上并不优于编码器-解码器架构，但后训练能提高上下文意识并减少男性先验偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在NLP任务中取得最先进成果，但在机器翻译中仍存在系统性偏见，特别是性别偏见。由于不同语言在性别标记上的系统性差异，翻译常需要将隐性的源语言信号消歧为显性的性别标记形式。现有基准测试可能捕捉到广泛差异，但未能反映现代机器翻译中性别偏见的全部复杂性。

Method: 扩展了现有的偏见评估框架：(1) 引入新的度量指标"先验偏见"，用于捕捉模型的默认性别假设；(2) 将该框架应用于仅解码器的机器翻译模型。

Result: 尽管仅解码器模型规模庞大且处于最先进水平，但在性别特定指标上通常不优于编码器-解码器架构。然而，后训练（如指令调优）不仅能提高上下文意识，还能减少男性先验偏见。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了更全面的性别偏见评估框架，揭示了仅解码器模型在性别偏见方面的局限性，并表明后训练是减少性别偏见、提高模型上下文意识的有效方法。

Abstract: While Large Language Models achieve state-of-the-art results across a wide range of NLP tasks, they remain prone to systematic biases. Among these, gender bias is particularly salient in MT, due to systematic differences across languages in whether and how gender is marked. As a result, translation often requires disambiguating implicit source signals into explicit gender-marked forms. In this context, standard benchmarks may capture broad disparities but fail to reflect the full complexity of gender bias in modern MT. In this paper, we extend recent frameworks on bias evaluation by: (i) introducing a novel measure coined "Prior Bias", capturing a model's default gender assumptions, and (ii) applying the framework to decoder-only MT models. Our results show that, despite their scale and state-of-the-art status, decoder-only models do not generally outperform encoder-decoder architectures on gender-specific metrics; however, post-training (e.g., instruction tuning) not only improves contextual awareness but also reduces the masculine Prior Bias.

</details>


### [45] [ConGA: Guidelines for Contextual Gender Annotation. A Framework for Annotating Gender in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17962)
*Argentina Anna Rescigno,Eva Vanmassenhove,Johanna Monti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了ConGA框架，用于解决英语到意大利语机器翻译中的性别偏见问题，通过细粒度标注和定量评估创建了评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译和大型语言模型在处理跨语言性别信息时面临持续挑战，特别是从性别中性语言（如英语）翻译到形态上有性别标记的语言（如意大利语）时。英语基本省略语法性别，而意大利语需要在多个语法范畴上保持性别一致。这种不对称性导致MT系统默认使用阳性形式，加剧了偏见并降低了翻译准确性。

Method: 提出了上下文性别标注（ConGA）框架，这是一套基于语言学的词级性别标注指南。该方案通过三个标签区分英语中的语义性别：阳性（M）、阴性（F）和模糊（A），以及意大利语中的语法性别实现：阳性（M）和阴性（F），并结合实体级标识符进行跨句子跟踪。将ConGA应用于gENder-IT数据集，创建了用于评估翻译中性别偏见的黄金标准资源。

Result: 研究结果揭示了系统性的阳性过度使用和不一致的阴性实现，突显了当前MT系统的持续局限性。通过结合细粒度语言标注和定量评估，这项工作为构建更具性别意识和多语言能力的NLP系统提供了方法论和基准。

Conclusion: ConGA框架通过提供系统的性别标注方法和评估基准，为解决机器翻译中的性别偏见问题提供了实用工具，有助于开发更公平、准确的多语言NLP系统。

Abstract: Handling gender across languages remains a persistent challenge for Machine Translation (MT) and Large Language Models (LLMs), especially when translating from gender-neutral languages into morphologically gendered ones, such as English to Italian. English largely omits grammatical gender, while Italian requires explicit agreement across multiple grammatical categories. This asymmetry often leads MT systems to default to masculine forms, reinforcing bias and reducing translation accuracy. To address this issue, we present the Contextual Gender Annotation (ConGA) framework, a linguistically grounded set of guidelines for word-level gender annotation. The scheme distinguishes between semantic gender in English through three tags, Masculine (M), Feminine (F), and Ambiguous (A), and grammatical gender realisation in Italian (Masculine (M), Feminine (F)), combined with entity-level identifiers for cross-sentence tracking. We apply ConGA to the gENder-IT dataset, creating a gold-standard resource for evaluating gender bias in translation. Our results reveal systematic masculine overuse and inconsistent feminine realisation, highlighting persistent limitations of current MT systems. By combining fine-grained linguistic annotation with quantitative evaluation, this work offers both a methodology and a benchmark for building more gender-aware and multilingual NLP systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 连续时间框架不仅减少分区复杂性，还提供对最优控制策略真实结构的更清晰理解，切换结构知识可加速在线优化方法，算法使其易于实际实现

Abstract: Model predictive control offers a powerful framework for managing constrained systems, but its repeated online optimization can become computationally prohibitive. Multiparametric programming addresses this challenge by precomputing optimal solutions offline, enabling real-time control through simple function evaluation. While extensively developed for discrete-time systems, this approach suffers from combinatorial growth in solution complexity as discretization is refined. This paper presents a systematic continuous-time multiparametric framework for linear-quadratic optimal control that directly solves Pontryagin's optimality conditions without discretization artifacts. Through two illustrative examples, we demonstrate that continuous-time formulations yield solutions with substantially fewer critical regions than their discrete-time counterparts. Beyond this reduction in partition complexity, the continuous-time approach provides deeper insight into system dynamics by explicitly identifying switching times and eliminating discretization artifacts that obscure the true structure of optimal control policies. Knowledge of the switching structure also accelerates online optimization methods by providing analytical information about the solution topology. Clear step-by-step algorithms are provided for identifying switching structures, computing parametric switching times, and constructing critical regions, making the continuous-time framework accessible for practical implementation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构造从包含初始条件的松弛集收缩到目标集的时间变化安全管。任何使该管保持前向不变的控制器都能保证规定时间恢复。收缩计划有界且可调，可行性条件简化为对收缩率的单一可验证条件，得到闭式最小恢复时间。

Result: 框架在16维多智能体系统和独轮车避障问题上验证有效，实现了规定时间恢复且控制努力有界。无论状态维度如何，每个时间步只施加单个仿射约束，可扩展到网格可达性方法难以处理的高维场景。

Conclusion: 提出的收缩CBF框架解决了规定时间控制中控制努力发散的问题，提供了有界可调的收缩计划，简化了输入约束下的可行性验证，并具有良好的可扩展性，适用于高维系统。

Abstract: We propose a constricting Control Barrier Function (CBF) framework for prescribed-time control of control-affine systems with input constraints. Given a system starting outside a target safe set, we construct a time-varying safety tube that shrinks from a relaxed set containing the initial condition to the target set at a user-specified deadline. Any controller rendering this tube forward invariant guarantees prescribed-time recovery by construction. The constriction schedule is bounded and tunable by design, in contrast to prescribed-time methods where control effort diverges near the deadline. Feasibility under input constraints reduces to a single verifiable condition on the constriction rate, yielding a closed-form minimum recovery time as a function of control authority and initial violation. The framework imposes a single affine constraint per timestep regardless of state dimension, scaling to settings where grid-based reachability methods are intractable. We validate on a 16-dimensional multi-agent system and a unicycle reach-avoid problem, demonstrating prescribed-time recovery with bounded control effort.

</details>

Result: 1. 证明非可识别性是普遍情况，即使归一化和多观测下兼容参数集通常也是多维的；2. 逆学习框架实现复杂度降低，独立于观测数量；3. 数值实验显示优越的解精度、更高的参数恢复率和显著的计算加速；4. 前瞻性可行性研究证明在血糖控制方面有改进。

Conclusion: 该研究为逆优化提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了传统参数恢复方法的根本局限性，提出的逆学习框架通过目标转移实现了复杂度降低，目标集成模型为平衡观测保真度和约束遵循提供了结构化方法，在个性化医疗等实际应用中展现出潜力。

Abstract: Inverse optimization seeks to recover unknown objective parameters from observed decisions, yet fundamental questions about when recovery is possible have received limited formal treatment. This paper develops a comprehensive theoretical framework for inverse optimization in parametric convex models. We first establish that non-identifiability is the generic case: even with normalization and multiple observations, the parameter set compatible with data is generically multi-dimensional, and regularization does not resolve this. We derive necessary and sufficient conditions for identifiability. Motivated by these negative results, we introduce the Inverse Learning (IL) framework, which shifts the inferential target from the unknown parameter to the latent optimal solution, achieving a complexity reduction that is independent of the number of observations. IL explicitly characterizes the full set of compatible parameters rather than returning an arbitrary element. To address the tension between observational fidelity and constraint adherence, we formalize the Observation-Constraint Tradeoff and develop Goal-Integrated Inverse Learning models that enable structured navigation of this spectrum with guaranteed monotonicity. Numerical experiments demonstrate superior solution accuracy, higher parameter recovery rates, and significant computational speedups. We apply the framework to personalized dietary recommendations using NHANES data, proof-of-concept demonstrating improved glycemic control in a prospective feasibility study.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of interpolation of smooth convex-concave functions. Interpolation is a key step for computer-assisted estimation of worst-case performance via PEP-like techniques, and smooth convex-concave functions are frequently used to model min-max problems arising in machine learning. We address the challenges associated with deriving conditions that are necessary and sufficient for the interpolation of smooth min-max games and show how existing approaches can be adapted to this setting.
  As part of this effort, we study the smoothing properties of Moreau-Yosida-like approximations of convex-concave functions. Next, we derive interpolation conditions for several key special cases of smooth min-max games. Finally, we obtain improved (i.e., tighter) characterizations for smooth strongly monotone convex-concave functions.
  We analyze the linear convergence of Alt-GDA using a PEP-like technique with novel constraints and (numerically) obtain a new estimate of its complexity. We are confident that the results of this paper provide meaningful progress toward establishing optimal worst-case guarantees for algorithms in the setting of smooth min-max games.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过数学分析识别出能够通过求解辅助凸问题获得全局最小化器的特殊情况。当直接方法不适用时，辅助问题的解仍可作为黎曼优化方法和自洽场算法的有效初始化，显著提升性能。

Result: 建立了在某些情况下全局最小化器可通过求解凸问题获得的数学条件。即使不满足这些条件，辅助凸问题的解也能为优化算法提供高质量初始化，改善收敛性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为Grassmann流形上的非凸优化问题提供了有效的数学分析和数值策略，特别适用于量子嵌入方法中的浴轨道构造问题，通过凸松弛和有效初始化显著提升了优化算法的性能。

Abstract: This article presents a mathematical analysis and numerical strategies for solving the optimization problem of minimizing the quadratic function $J(P) = \text{Tr}(BP)- \frac{1}{2} \text{Tr}(A P A P)$, where $A,B \in \mathbb R^{M \times M}_{\rm sym}$, with $A \succeq 0$, over the Grassmann manifold ${\rm Gr}(m,\mathbb R^M)$. While this problem is non-convex and typically admits non-global local minima - posing challenges for Riemannian optimization and self-consistent field (SCF) algorithms - we identify cases where the global minimizer can be obtained by solving an auxiliary convex problem. When this approach is not directly applicable, the solution to the auxiliary problem still serves as an effective initialization for Riemannian optimization methods and SCF algorithms, significantly improving their performance. This work is motivated by applications in quantum embedding methods, particularly in the construction of bath orbitals, where such optimization problems naturally arise.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Debreu-Koopmans定理限制可分离聚合最多只能有一个非凸分量，这限制了经济学建模的灵活性。作者旨在突破这一限制，为更广泛的经济模型提供理论基础。

Method: 证明了可分离（加法或乘法）函数是星拟凸的当且仅当每个分量都是星拟凸的。建立了完整的微积分体系，包括单调复合、逐点最小值、拟算术平均等运算规则。

Result: 获得了可分离拟凸函数和的星拟凸性，将分散化理论与前景理论的S形价值函数形式化地联系起来。应用包括Cobb-Douglas函数、多因素风险模型和去中心化金融中的恒定函数做市商。

Conclusion: 星拟凸性为超越经典Debreu-Koopmans约束的经济建模提供了统一框架，支持更灵活的函数形式，能够更好地描述现实经济行为。

Abstract: The Debreu Koopmans theorem restricts separable aggregation to at most one nonconvex component. We solve this by proving that a separable, additive or multiplicative, function is star quasiconvex, those with star shaped sublevel sets about minimizers, if and only if each component is star quasiconvex. This immediately yields star quasiconvexity of separable sums of quasiconvex functions, formally bridging diversification theory with the S shaped value functions of Prospect Theory. Furthermore, we develop a complete calculus, monotonic composition, pointwise minima, quasi-arithmetic means, and we apply it to Cobb-Douglas functions, multifactor risk models, and constant function market makers in decentralized finance. Star quasiconvexity thus provides a unified framework for economic modeling beyond the classical Debreu Koopmans constraint.

</details>

Abstract: We apply proof mining techniques to obtain quantitative and qualitative results on asymptotic and T-asymptotic regularity for the inexact generalized Halpern iteration, a viscosity-type extension of an iteration recently studied by Kanzow and Shehu.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然可以多项式时间计算足够大且正确的big-M参数，但验证给定big-M参数是否不会排除任何可行或最优下层解是计算困难的。本文旨在建立验证big-M参数正确性的计算复杂性理论结果。

Method: 通过理论分析建立两个互补的硬度结果：1) 即使只有一个可能不正确的big-M参数，验证所得MILP模型最优解是否为双层最优是coNP完全的；2) 即使MILP模型的最优解已知，验证全局big-M正确性仍然是计算困难的。

Result: 证明了两个关键硬度结果：首先，即使只有一个可能不正确的big-M参数，验证MILP最优解是否为双层最优是coNP完全的，这一负结果适用于无耦合约束的min-max问题以及基于强对偶的混合整数双层规划重构。其次，即使已知MILP模型的最优解，验证全局big-M正确性仍然是计算困难的。

Conclusion: 验证big-M参数正确性在计算上是困难的，即使在后验情况下也是如此。这些结果强调了在双层规划中使用big-M参数时需要谨慎，并揭示了相关验证问题的计算复杂性。

Abstract: A standard approach to solving optimistic bilevel linear programs (BLPs) is to replace the lower-level problem with its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions and reformulate the resulting complementarity constraints using auxiliary binary variables. This yields a single-level mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model involving big-$M$ parameters. While sufficiently large and bilevel-correct big-$M$s can be computed in polynomial time, verifying a priori that given big-$M$s do not cut off any feasible or optimal lower-level solutions is known to be computationally difficult. In this paper, we establish two complementary hardness results. First, we show that, even with a single potentially incorrect big-$M$ parameter, it is $coNP$-complete to verify a posteriori whether the optimal solution of the resulting MILP model is bilevel optimal. In particular, this negative result persists for min-max problems without coupling constraints and applies to strong-duality-based reformulations of mixed-integer BLPs. Second, we show that verifying global big-$M$ correctness remains computationally difficult a posteriori, even when an optimal solution of the MILP model is available.

</details>

*Daniel Zelazo,Louis Theran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究异构控制仿射非线性智能体通过扩散相对输出测量的同步问题，提出基于边空间和提升的两步设计方法，引入可容许性概念并建立图拓扑与驱动结构对可实现边动态的约束关系。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异构控制仿射非线性智能体在扩散相对输出测量下的同步问题，受最近形成控制的几何边空间公式启发，旨在开发系统化设计框架，分析图拓扑和驱动结构如何共同限制可实现的边动态。

Method: 将设计分为边空间步骤和提升步骤：边空间步骤指定相对输出的稳定模型演化，提升步骤利用控制仿射几何下智能体允许的输入方向实现规定的边运动。引入可容许性概念，将通用可容许性与关联二分图的结构化秩和最大匹配联系起来，提供可检查的组合证书。

Result: 建立了可容许性概念，表明图拓扑和驱动结构如何共同限制可实现的边动态。通过关联二分图的结构化秩和最大匹配提供了可检查的组合证书，并在非线性振荡器同步问题上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 提出了异构控制仿射非线性智能体同步的两步设计框架，建立了图拓扑与驱动结构对可实现边动态的约束关系，为分布式控制系统设计提供了理论工具和组合验证方法。

Abstract: We study synchronization of heterogeneous control-affine nonlinear agents interconnected through diffusive relative-output measurements. Motivated by recent geometric edge-space formulations for formation control, we separate the design into an \emph{edge-space} step, which specifies a stabilizing model evolution for relative outputs, and a \emph{lift} step, which realizes the prescribed edge motion using the agents' allowable input directions under the control-affine geometry. We introduce an admissibility notion that characterizes when an edge-driven diffusive design is feasible. We relate generic admissibility to structured rank and maximum matchings in an associated bipartite graph, yielding checkable combinatorial certificates that show how graph topology and actuation structure jointly limit achievable edge dynamics. The results are illustrated on synchronization of nonlinear oscillators.

</details>

Result: 证明在收入中性约束下，近视政策（每期最大化可再生能源容量）是条件最优的，能够实现长期最优。展示了不同约束条件和决策变量边际价值对应能源转型的不同阶段。研究表明，电力公司应战略性地设计收入分配决策，平衡可再生能源扩张投资和对现有可再生能源机组的补贴。

Abstract: This paper develops a multi-period optimization framework to design a voluntary renewable program (VRP) for an electric utility company, aiming to maximize total renewable energy deployments. In the business model of VRP, the utility must ensure it generates renewable energy up to the total amount of contract during each market episode (i.e., a year), while all the revenue collected from the VRP must either be used to invest in procuring renewable capacities or to maintain the current renewable fleet and infrastructure. We thus formulate the problem as an optimal pricing problem coupled with revenue allocation and renewable deployment decisions. We model the demand function of voluntary renewable contracts as an exponential decay function based on survey data. We analytically derive the optimal pricing policy of the VRP as a function of the current grid carbon intensity. We prove that a myopic policy is conditionally optimal, which maximizes renewable capacity in each period, attains the long-run optimum due to the utility's revenue-neutral constraint. We show different binding conditions and marginal values of decision variables correspond to different phases of the energy transition, and that the utility should strategically design its revenue-sharing decisions, balancing investments in renewable expansion and subsidizing existing renewable fleets. Finally, we show that voluntary renewable programs can only extend renewable penetration but cannot achieve net-zero emissions or a fully renewable grid. This pricing-allocation-expansion framework highlights both the potential and limitations of voluntary renewable demand, providing analytical insight into optimal policy design and the qualitative shifts occurring during the energy transition process.

</details>

*Dian Jin,Jeremy Coulson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明了行为系统子空间预测器在基变换下的不变性，并推导了首个基于行为距离的显式预测误差界，表明预测器对行为扰动具有局部Lipschitz性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为系统通过轨迹集合定义离散时间线性时不变系统，这些轨迹形成线性子空间。实践中通常用数据矩阵表示这些子空间，但对于鲁棒性认证和不确定性量化，矩阵表示依赖于坐标系，缺乏坐标无关的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 首先证明子空间预测器在基变换下的不变性，仅依赖于底层行为子空间。然后推导首个基于真实子空间与估计之间行为距离的显式预测误差界，证明预测器对行为扰动具有局部Lipschitz性质。还提出适用于滚动时域实现的一步预测误差界。

Result: 理论分析表明：1) 子空间预测器具有坐标不变性；2) 预测误差界可由行为距离上界；3) 平均预测误差界随行为距离线性增长。数值实验验证了理论界确实上界预测误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为行为系统数据驱动预测和控制提供了坐标无关的不确定性量化框架，建立了子空间预测器与行为距离之间的理论联系，为鲁棒性分析提供了数学基础。

Abstract: Behavioral systems define discrete-time Linear Time-Invariant systems in terms of a set of trajectories, which forms a linear subspace. This subspace underlies the subspace predictor used in data-driven prediction and control. In practice, such subspaces are typically represented through data matrices. For robustness certification and uncertainty quantification, however, these matrix representations are coordinate-dependent and therefore do not provide a coordinate-free way to quantify uncertainty. In this work, we establish two key properties of the subspace predictor. We first show that the subspace predictor is invariant under change of basis and depends only on the underlying behavioral subspace. We then derive the first explicit prediction error bound in terms of behavioral distance between the true subspace and an estimate, showing that the predictor is locally Lipschitz with respect to behavioral perturbations. We also present a one-step prediction error bound that is relevant for receding-horizon implementations and can be computed directly. Numerical experiments show that the theoretical bound upper-bounds the prediction error, and that the average prediction error bound grows linearly with the behavioral distance.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的决策缩放方法，用于解决罕见事件场景下机会约束优化的计算可行性问题，相比传统场景方法显著减少样本需求

Method: 提出决策缩放方法，仅使用原始数据样本和单个标量超参数来缩放约束，使其适用于标准求解器。基于大偏差原理，在满足常见分布族的温和非参数假设下工作

Conclusion: 决策缩放方法显著扩展了可高效可靠解决的问题范围，在金融和工程应用中表现出色

Abstract: Chance-constrained optimization is a suitable modeling framework for safety-critical applications where violating constraints is nearly unacceptable. The scenario approach is a popular solution method for these problems, due to its straightforward implementation and ability to preserve problem structure. However, in the rare-event regime where constraint violations must be kept extremely unlikely, the scenario approach becomes computationally infeasible due to the excessively large sample sizes it demands. We address this limitation with a new yet straightforward decision-scaling method that relies exclusively on original data samples and a single scalar hyperparameter that scales the constraints in a way amenable to standard solvers. Our method leverages large deviation principles under mild nonparametric assumptions satisfied by commonly used distribution families in practice. For a broad class of problems satisfying certain practically verifiable structural assumptions, the method achieves a polynomial reduction in sample size requirements compared to the classical scenario approach, while also guaranteeing asymptotic feasibility in the rare-event regime. Numerical experiments spanning finance and engineering applications show that our decision-scaling method significantly expands the scope of problems that can be solved both efficiently and reliably.

</details>

Result: 为线性系统和仿射CBF约束提供了分析条件，能够确定闭环系统是否会出现不期望的平衡点、无界轨迹，或原点是否具有全局指数稳定性

Conclusion: 该研究解决了CBF安全滤波器动力学特性分析的重要问题，为线性系统和仿射CBF约束提供了理论分析框架，有助于理解和设计更可靠的安全滤波器

Abstract: This letter studies the dynamical properties of safety filters designed based on Control Barrier Functions (CBF). This mechanism, which is popular in safety-critical applications, takes a nominal controller and minimally modifies it to render it safe. Although CBF-based safety filters make the closed-loop system safe, characterizing their additional dynamical properties, such as stability, boundedness, or existence of spurious equilibria, remains a challenging problem. Here, we address this problem for the case of linear systems and an affine CBF constraint. We provide conditions under which the closed-loop system presents undesired equilibria, unbounded trajectories, or the origin is globally exponentially stable.

</details>


### [59] [An error bound-based convergence analysis framework for a class of randomized algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17438)
*Zhichun Yang,Li Jiang,Tianxiang Liu,Man-Chung Yue*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一误差界框架分析随机算法，涵盖坐标下降、投影方法等，提供非渐进期望收敛和渐进几乎必然收敛保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于误差界的随机算法分析范围有限且收敛保证过于保守，需要开发更通用、精确的分析框架

Result: 在全局UEB条件下获得非渐进期望收敛和渐进几乎必然收敛率；在局部UEB条件下获得渐进几乎必然收敛率；应用于固定点问题和光滑可定义函数最小化

Conclusion: 统一误差界框架为随机算法提供了更通用、精确的收敛分析工具，扩展了现有理论并应用于多个重要问题

Abstract: Existing error-bound-based analyses for stochastic algorithms that exhibit certain descent properties, such as randomized coordinate descent and randomized projection methods, are often limited in scope and typically lead to overly conservative convergence guarantees. To address this gap, we develop an abstract framework for analyzing such stochastic algorithms based on new unified error bound (UEB) conditions. The proposed UEB conditions subsume many common error bound- and Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz-type conditions used in existing studies of algorithms for optimization, convex feasibility, and common fixed point problems. Under the global UEB condition, we establish non-asymptotic in-expectation and asymptotic almost-sure convergence rates for the stochastic algorithms in our framework. Under the local UEB condition, we also show asymptotic almost sure convergence rates. We demonstrate the strength and versatility of our framework through two applications. For the common fixed point problem, we provide comprehensive convergence guarantees for the randomized alternating Krasnoselskii-Mann method under Hölderian error bound conditions. Furthermore, for unconstrained minimization of smooth definable functions, we establish novel convergence guarantees for the randomized subspace descent method, an algorithm subsuming both randomized coordinate and block coordinate descent.

</details>


### [60] [Hierarchical Stability Notions and Lyapunov Functions for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17536)
*Matthew M. Peet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于层次化分类偏微分方程稳定性概念和Lyapunov条件的框架，通过定义基本状态来简化稳定性结果的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性偏微分方程存在多种不等价的稳定性概念，这使得Lyapunov稳定性结果的解释变得复杂。为了简化这种解释，需要建立一个统一的框架来层次化分类稳定性概念和Lyapunov条件。

Method: 对于每个适定的PDE和边界条件集，定义L2空间上的基本状态，该状态对应唯一前向传播解所需的最小信息。稳定性概念和Lyapunov函数都基于这个基本状态定义，从而建立稳定性概念的层次结构。

Result: 建立了稳定性概念的层次结构，最弱的是基本状态到PDE状态的稳定性。其他稳定性概念和Lyapunov条件可以相对于这个最弱概念进行解释。为Lyapunov稳定性、指数稳定性和有限能量稳定性建立了层次结构，并通过算子不等式定义了充分的Lyapunov条件。

Conclusion: 提出的框架简化了PDE稳定性结果的解释，通过层次化分类稳定性概念和Lyapunov条件，使得不同稳定性概念之间的关系更加清晰。提供了说明性示例和计算工具来支持该框架的应用。

Abstract: Unlike linear ordinary differential equations (ODEs), linear partial differential equations (PDEs) admit a multitude of non-equivalent notions of stability. This variety makes interpretation of Lyapunov stability results challenging. To simplify this interpretation, we propose a framework for hierarchical classification of notions of stability and Lyapunov conditions. To do this, for every well-posed PDE and set of boundary conditions, we define a fundamental state on $L_2$ corresponding to the minimal information needed to uniquely forward propagate the solution. Stability notions and Lyapunov functions are then defined in terms of this fundamental state. This gives rise to a hierarchy of stability notions, the weakest being fundamental state to PDE state stability. Other stability notions and Lyapunov conditions may then be interpreted relative to this weakest notion. Hierarchies are established for: Lyapunov, exponential and finite-energy stability. Sufficient Lyapunov conditions are defined in terms of operator inequalities. Illustrative examples and computational tools are provided.

</details>


### [61] [Complex Markets and Mean Field Games: Beyond Basic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17539)
*Agustín Muñoz González*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在已有均值场博弈框架基础上，提出了一个更完整的流动性池模型，整合了交易者、流动性提供者和套利者三类参与者，构建了Major-Minor博弈系统，但未提供存在性证明，属于开放研究提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有均值场博弈框架（无交易成本和含交易成本）基础上，构建更完整的流动性池模型，整合三类关键参与者（交易者、流动性提供者、套利者），为理解去中心化交易所中复杂的策略互动提供更全面的理论框架。

Method: 1. 将流动性提供者建模为主导玩家（Bensoussan等人的主导玩家均值场博弈框架）；2. 交易者群体用均值场分布描述；3. 套利者采用Angeris等人的优化问题模型；4. 套利者对流动性提供者的影响通过Milionis等人的损失与再平衡指标量化；5. 构建三类参与者耦合的Major-Minor博弈系统。

Result: 提出了一个完整的三方参与者模型框架，识别了主要技术难点（如均衡存在性证明），为后续研究奠定了基础。明确说明本文是开放研究提案而非封闭结果集合。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析去中心化交易所中复杂策略互动提供了更全面的理论框架，将流动性提供者作为主导玩家、交易者作为均值场、套利者作为优化者的整合方法具有创新性，但需要进一步研究解决均衡存在性等技术难题。

Abstract: This work builds on the theoretical frameworks presented in "Liquidity pools as mean field games: A new framework" and "Liquidity pools as mean field games with transaction costs" by the same author, where the strategic interactions among traders in a constant-product market-making protocol were modelled using mean field games (MFG), first without transaction costs and then incorporating them. Here we present the formulation of a more complete model that integrates three types of agents: traders, liquidity providers (LPs), and arbitrageurs. While we do not establish existence results for this general model, the formulation identifies the main technical difficulties and lays the groundwork for future work. The LP acts as a dominating player in the sense of 'Mean field games with a dominating player' by Bensoussan, Chau, and Yam: its strategy influences the mean field distribution of the traders, and the equilibrium is sought as a solution to the coupled system of three problems that constitute a Major-Minor game. The arbitrageurs operate by solving the optimization problem presented in "An analysis of uniswap markets" by Angeris et al., and their impact on the LP is captured through the loss-versus-rebalancing of "Automated market making and loss-versus-rebalancing" by Milionis et al. The material in this article should be read as an open research proposal rather than a collection of closed results.

</details>


### [62] [Data-Driven Estimation of Vinnicombe metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17545)
*Margarita A. Guerrero,Henrik Sandberg,Cristian R. Rojas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种无需辨识、直接从输入输出数据估计离散时间SISO系统间ν-gap的数据驱动方法，并基于Welch型平均提供验证拓扑条件的数据驱动环绕数测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在鲁棒控制中，以控制相关的方式量化模型失配至关重要。ν-gap（Vinnicombe度量）是从闭环角度衡量名义模型与真实系统差异的著名度量，但其计算通常需要真实系统的显式知识。

Method: 提出一种无需辨识的数据驱动方法，直接从输入输出实验数据估计离散时间SISO系统间的ν-gap。方法包含基于Welch型平均的数据驱动环绕数测试，用于验证计算该度量所需的拓扑条件。

Result: 在重型燃气轮机模型和教科书示例上的数值仿真表明，所提出的估计与MATLAB的gapmetric函数结果非常接近，同时能正确检测出可容许条件失败的情况。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种无需系统辨识即可从实验数据直接估计ν-gap的有效途径，并具备验证拓扑条件的能力，为数据驱动的鲁棒控制分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Quantifying model mismatch in a control-relevant manner is fundamental in robust control. A well-known metric for this purpose is the $ν$-gap, or Vinnicombe metric, which measures the discrepancy between a nominal model and the real system from a closed-loop viewpoint. However, its computation typically requires explicit knowledge of the true system. In this letter, we propose an identification-free, data-driven method to estimate the $ν$-gap between discrete-time SISO systems directly from input-output experiments. The method is complemented by a data-driven winding-number test, based on Welch-type averaging, to verify a required topological condition for the computation of the metric. Numerical simulations on heavy-duty gas-turbine models and a textbook example show that the proposed estimate closely matches MATLAB$^©$ \texttt{gapmetric}, while correctly detecting cases in which the admissibility conditions fail.

</details>


### [63] [Stochastic set-valued optimization and its application to robust learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17691)
*Tommaso Giovannelli,Jingfu Tan,Luis Nunes Vicente*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出随机集值优化框架用于鲁棒机器学习，将决策变量映射到目标值集合，通过集合关系定义最优性，特别关注超盒集值优化问题，可重构为有限目标的多目标优化问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验风险最小化和经典鲁棒模型无法充分捕捉损失分布的下尾和上尾行为，需要新的优化框架来提供可解释的权衡，在分布偏移下实现更好的鲁棒性和更低的变异性。

Method: 开发随机集值优化框架，专注于超盒集值优化问题，将其重构为有限目标的多目标优化问题；构建包含分位数和超分位数的随机区间/矩形值优化公式；采用随机多梯度算法求解多目标问题，并选择帕累托膝部解。

Conclusion: 随机集值优化框架为鲁棒机器学习提供了新方法，通过捕捉损失分布的双尾行为超越传统方法，在分布偏移下实现更好的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a stochastic set-valued optimization (SVO) framework tailored for robust machine learning. In the SVO setting, each decision variable is mapped to a set of objective values, and optimality is defined via set relations. We focus on SVO problems with hyperbox sets, which can be reformulated as multi-objective optimization (MOO) problems with finitely many objectives and serve as a foundation for representing or approximating more general mapped sets. Two special cases of hyperbox-valued optimization (HVO) are interval-valued (IVO) and rectangle-valued (RVO) optimization. We construct stochastic IVO/RVO formulations that incorporate subquantiles and superquantiles into the objective functions of the MOO reformulations, providing a new characterization for subquantiles. These formulations provide interpretable trade-offs by capturing both lower- and upper-tail behaviors of loss distributions, thereby going beyond standard empirical risk minimization and classical robust models. To solve the resulting multi-objective problems, we adopt stochastic multi-gradient algorithms and select a Pareto knee solution. In numerical experiments, the proposed algorithms with this selection strategy exhibit improved robustness and reduced variability across test replications under distributional shift compared with empirical risk minimization, while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [64] [Certainty-equivalent adaptive MPC for uncertain nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17843)
*Johannes Köhler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合模型预测控制与参数自适应的方法，为非线性系统设计具有强鲁棒性能保证的自适应控制器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为非线性系统设计自适应控制器，在存在测量噪声、干扰和参数变化的情况下，提供强鲁棒性能保证，同时考虑执行器限制和状态约束

Method: 将确定性等价MPC与最小均方参数自适应相结合，利用有限时域滚动优化、人工参考、优化控制器分析技术和软状态约束，无需系统特定的离线设计

Result: 对于开环稳定系统获得半全局结果，适用于任意大的测量噪声、干扰和参数不确定性；对于可镇定系统获得区域结果，在给定吸引域内且不确定性足够小时有效

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性系统自适应控制提供了理论保证，通过数值仿真验证了其在具有大参数不确定性系统（线性稳定质量-弹簧-阻尼器链和非线性不稳定四旋翼避障）中的适用性和优势

Abstract: We provide a method to design adaptive controllers for nonlinear systems using model predictive control (MPC). By combining a certainty-equivalent MPC formulation with least-mean-square parameter adaptation, we obtain an adaptive controller with strong robust performance guarantees: The cumulative tracking error and violation of state constraints scale linearly with noise energy, disturbance energy, and path length of parameter variation. A key technical contribution is developing the underlying certainty-equivalent MPC that tracks output references, accounts for actuator limitations and desired state constraints, requires no system-specific offline design, and provides strong inherent robustness properties. This is achieved by leveraging finite-horizon rollouts, artificial references, recent analysis techniques for optimization-based controllers, and soft state constraints. For open-loop stable systems, we derive a semi-global result that applies to arbitrarily large measurement noise, disturbances, and parametric uncertainty. For stabilizable systems, we derive a regional result that is valid within a given region of attraction and for sufficiently small uncertainty. Applicability and benefits are demonstrated with numerical simulations involving systems with large parametric uncertainty: a linear stable chain of mass-spring-dampers and a nonlinear unstable quadrotor navigating obstacles.

</details>


### [65] [Minimum-Energy Control For Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17933)
*Cyprien Tamekue,Zongxi Yu,ShiNung Ching*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于拉格朗日乘子不动点方程和非对称Gramian类矩阵的最小能量控制方法，适用于仿射控制系统，具有阶乘衰减收敛性和可扩展性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广泛的仿射控制系统开发一种可实现的、高度可扩展的最小能量控制综合方法，该方法具有内在的能量界限保证

Method: 使用拉格朗日乘子不动点方程和一般非对称的Gramian类矩阵推导最小能量控制；在可行的强制性类中，该不动点是唯一的，可通过标准Picard迭代计算；迭代具有阶乘衰减收敛性

Result: 方法具有可实现的、高度可扩展的合成特性，并带有内在能量界限；通过线性时变系统的均匀完全可控性结果，推导出带标量输入的时变平面仿射控制系统的括号生成条件；特别处理了独轮车运动学模型，数值示例验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的最小能量控制综合框架，适用于广泛的仿射控制系统，具有理论保证和实际可实现性，为时变平面控制系统提供了可控性条件，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性

Abstract: In this letter, we derive minimum-energy controls for a broad class of control-affine systems using a Lagrange multiplier fixed-point equation and a generally non-symmetric Gramian-like matrix. In feasible coercivity classes, this fixed point is unique and can be computed by standard Picard iteration. These iterates converge with factorial decay, yielding an implementable, highly scalable synthesis with an intrinsic energy bound. As a demonstration of concept, we use uniform complete controllability results for linear time-varying systems to derive a bracket-generating condition ensuring complete controllability for time-dependent planar control-affine systems with scalar inputs. Special treatment for the unicycle kinematic model is also provided, and numerical examples illustrate the approach's effectiveness.

</details>

### [66] [A foundation model for electrodermal activity data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16878)
*Leonardo Alchieri,Matteo Garzon,Lidia Alecci,Francesco Bombassei De Bona,Martin Gjoreski,Giovanni De Felice,Silvia Santini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个专门针对皮肤电活动（EDA）的基础模型UME，基于新构建的大规模公开数据集EDAMAME（24个数据集，634用户，25,000+小时数据）训练，在10个场景中8个表现优于基线，计算资源消耗仅为通用时序基础模型的1/20。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤电活动（EDA）作为反映交感神经系统活动的重要生理信号，在认知负荷、压力、参与度等评估中广泛应用，但缺乏大规模、经过整理、公开可访问的数据集阻碍了该领域基础模型的发展。当前仅有的一个大规模档案是专有的，且很少有可穿戴设备能提供连续、无干扰的传感。

Method: 1. 构建EDAMAME数据集：从24个公开数据集中收集EDA数据，包含634名用户的25,000多小时数据；2. 基于此资源训练UME模型，这是首个专门针对EDA的基础模型；3. 在十个不同场景下评估模型性能，并与基线方法和通用时序基础模型进行比较。

Result: 在十个评估场景中，UME在八个场景中表现优于基线方法，且与通用时序基础模型性能相当，同时计算资源消耗仅为后者的1/20。研究也揭示了EDA建模的内在挑战，表明该领域仍有进一步研究的空间。

Conclusion: 通过构建首个大规模公开EDA数据集EDAMAME和训练首个专门EDA基础模型UME，本研究填补了该领域的空白。虽然模型表现出色且计算效率高，但研究也强调了EDA建模的固有挑战，需要进一步研究以充分发挥其潜力。所有数据集、模型权重和代码均已公开以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Foundation models have recently extended beyond natural language and vision to timeseries domains, including physiological signals. However, progress in electrodermal activity (EDA) modeling is hindered by the absence of large-scale, curated, and openly accessible datasets. EDA reflects sympathetic nervous system activity and is widely used to infer cognitive load, stress, and engagement. Yet very few wearable devices provide continuous, unobtrusive sensing, and the only large-scale archive to date is proprietary. To address this gap, we compile EDAMAME, a collection of EDA traces from 24 public datasets, comprising more than 25,000 hours from 634 users. Using this resource, we train UME, the first dedicated foundation model for EDA. In eight out of ten scenarios, UME outperforms baselines and matches generalist timeseries foundation models while using 20x fewer computational resources. Our findings, however, also highlight the intrinsic challenges of EDA modeling, motivating further research to unlock its full potential. All datasets, model weights, and code are released to support further research.

</details>


### [67] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing: Balancing Profitability,Stability and Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16888)
*Krishna Kumar Neelakanta Pillai Santha Kumari Amma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了多智能体强化学习（MAPPO和MADDPG）在竞争性零售市场动态定价中的应用，发现MAPPO在利润和稳定性方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性零售市场的动态定价需要能够适应需求波动和竞争对手行为的策略，现有独立学习方法可能无法充分处理多智能体交互的复杂性。

Method: 使用基于真实零售数据的模拟市场环境，系统评估MAPPO和MADDPG算法，以独立DDPG（IDDPG）作为基准，从利润表现、随机种子稳定性、公平性和训练效率四个维度进行对比。

Result: MAPPO在平均收益方面表现最佳且方差低，提供了稳定可复现的竞争性定价方案；MADDPG利润略低但实现了最公平的利润分配；两种MARL方法均优于独立学习基准。

Conclusion: 多智能体强化学习方法（特别是MAPPO）为动态零售定价提供了可扩展且稳定的替代方案，优于传统的独立学习方法。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in competitive retail markets requires strategies that adapt to fluctuating demand and competitor behavior. In this work, we present a systematic empirical evaluation of multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) approaches-specifically MAPPO and MADDPG-for dynamic price optimization under competition. Using a simulated marketplace environment derived from real-world retail data, we benchmark these algorithms against an Independent DDPG (IDDPG) baseline, a widely used independent learner in MARL literature. We evaluate profit performance, stability across random seeds, fairness, and training efficiency. Our results show that MAPPO consistently achieves the highest average returns with low variance, offering a stable and reproducible approach for competitive price optimization, while MADDPG achieves slightly lower profit but the fairest profit distribution among agents. These findings demonstrate that MARL methods-particularly MAPPO-provide a scalable and stable alternative to independent learning approaches for dynamic retail pricing.

</details>


### [68] [What on Earth is AlphaEarth? Hierarchical structure and functional interpretability for global land cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16911)
*Ivan Felipe Benavides-Martinez,Justin Guthrie,Jhon Edwin Arias,Yeison Alberto Garces-Gomez,Angela Ines Guzman-Alvis,Cristiam Victoriano Portilla-Cabrera,Somnath Mondal,Andrew J. Allyn,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种功能解释性框架，通过分析嵌入维度对土地覆盖分类的贡献，揭示了AlphaEarth嵌入空间的功能层次结构，发现仅需2-12个维度即可达到98%的基线性能，证明了嵌入空间的高度冗余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型生成的高维嵌入虽具有强大预测性能，但其内部组织机制不明确，限制了科学应用。现有研究仅将嵌入与连续环境变量关联，但嵌入空间是否存在功能或层次组织（如某些维度作为专门表示，其他维度编码共享或更广泛的地理空间结构）仍不清楚。

Method: 提出功能解释性框架，通过观察分类行为逆向工程嵌入维度的功能角色。结合大规模实验和基于特征重要性模式及渐进消融的结构分析，研究嵌入-类别关系。通过特征重要性模式识别维度对特定土地覆盖类别的贡献，并通过渐进消融评估维度冗余性。

Result: 嵌入维度表现出一致且非均匀的功能行为，可沿功能谱系分类：与特定土地覆盖类别相关的专家维度、捕获类别间共享特征的低中层次通才维度、反映更广泛环境梯度的高层次通才维度。关键发现：仅需2-12个维度（共64个）即可达到98%的基线分类性能，表明嵌入空间存在显著冗余。

Conclusion: AlphaEarth嵌入不仅具有物理信息性，而且按功能组织成层次结构。这为操作分类任务中的维度选择提供了实用指导，展示了通过维度选择显著降低计算成本的途径，增强了地理空间基础模型的可解释性和实用性。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models generate high-dimensional embeddings that achieve strong predictive performance, yet their internal organization remains obscure, limiting their scientific use. Recent interpretability studies relate Google AlphaEarth Foundations (GAEF) embeddings to continuous environmental variables, but it is still unclear whether the embedding space exhibits a functional or hierarchical organization, in which some dimensions act as specialized representations while others encode shared or broader geospatial structure. In this work, we propose a functional interpretability framework that reverse-engineers the role of embedding dimensions by characterizing their contribution to land cover structure from observed classification behavior. The approach combines large-scale experimentation with a structural analysis of embedding-class relationships based on feature importance patterns and progressive ablation. Our results show that embedding dimensions exhibit consistent and non-uniform functional behavior, allowing them to be categorized along a hierarchical functional spectrum: specialist dimensions associated with specific land cover classes, low- and mid-generalist dimensions capturing shared characteristics between classes, and highgeneralist dimensions reflecting broader environmental gradients. Critically, we find that accurate land cover classification (98% of baseline performance) can be achieved using as few as 2 to 12 of the 64 available dimensions, depending on the class. This demonstrates substantial redundancy in the embedding space and offers a pathway toward significant reductions in computational cost. Together, these findings reveal that AlphaEarth embeddings are not only physically informative, but also functionally organized into a hierarchical structure, providing practical guidance for dimension selection in operational classification tasks.

</details>


### [69] [HoloByte: Continuous Hyperspherical Distillation for Tokenizer-Free Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16917)
*Vladimer Khasia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HoloByte：基于连续超球面蒸馏的无分词器序列建模框架，通过将字节序列投影到连续超球面流形，将注意力复杂度从O(N²D)降低到O(N²/W²D + ND²)，在参数约束相同的情况下优于BPE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列建模依赖离散子词分词来规避字节级注意力的O(N²)计算不可行性，但这种启发式量化引入了人工形态边界、强制词汇表依赖，并破坏了优化景观的连续性。

Method: HoloByte采用连续超球面蒸馏：1）将离散字节序列分割为固定容量块；2）通过可逆、维度保持的正交旋转算子将其投影到连续有界超球面流形；3）宏观Transformer在压缩连续表示上操作；4）局部因果微解码器解绑表示计算精确字节级分布；5）采用双目标公式，包含数学精确的全息潜在均方误差，严格约束梯度并保证渐近稳定性。

Result: 理论上推导出确保从连续流形无错误离散恢复所需的最小嵌入维度D = Ω(W ln|V|)。实证上，在严格匹配的参数约束下，HoloByte系统性地优于可比较的离散字节对编码（BPE）基线。

Conclusion: 连续超球面蒸馏为词汇表不变的序列建模提供了数学严谨且计算可行的基础，解决了离散分词与连续优化之间的二分法问题。

Abstract: Sequence modeling universally relies on discrete subword tokenization to circumvent the $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ computational intractability of native byte-level attention. However, this heuristic quantization imposes artificial morphological boundaries, enforces vocabulary dependence, and fractures the continuity of the optimization landscape. To resolve this dichotomy, we introduce \textbf{HoloByte}: a strictly tokenizer-free framework utilizing Continuous Hyperspherical Distillation. HoloByte partitions discrete byte sequences into fixed-capacity chunks and projects them into a continuous, strictly bounded hyperspherical manifold via an invertible, dimension-preserving orthogonal rotation operator. This spatial superposition allows a macroscopic transformer to operate exclusively on compressed continuous representations, formally reducing the exact attention time complexity from $\mathcal{O}(N^2D)$ to $\mathcal{O}\left( \frac{N^2}{W^2}D + ND^2 \right)$. A localized causal micro-decoder subsequently unbinds these representations to compute exact byte-level distributions. To govern this continuous trajectory, we propose a dual-objective formulation incorporating a mathematically precise Holographic Latent Mean Squared Error, which strictly bounds the gradient and guarantees asymptotic stability. Theoretically, we derive the minimal embedding dimension $D = Ω(W \ln |\mathcal{V}|)$ required to ensure error-free discrete recovery from the continuous manifold. Empirically, under strictly matched parameter constraints, HoloByte is systematically outperforming a comparable discrete Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) baseline. These results establish Continuous Hyperspherical Distillation as a mathematically rigorous and computationally tractable foundation for vocabulary-invariant sequence modeling. The code is available at https://github.com/VladimerKhasia/HoloByte

</details>


### [70] [MHPO: Modulated Hazard-aware Policy Optimization for Stable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16929)
*Hongjun Wang,Wei Liu,Weibo Gu,Xing Sun,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MHPO提出了一种新的强化学习框架，通过Log-Fidelity调制器和解耦危险惩罚机制，解决了GRPO中重要性比率调节的梯度保真度和危险感知问题，显著提升了训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO框架中的重要性比率控制方法（如硬裁剪）存在非可微边界和梯度消失区域，无法保持梯度保真度，且缺乏危险感知机制来自适应抑制极端偏差，使优化过程易受突发策略偏移的影响。

Method: 提出MHPO框架：1) Log-Fidelity调制器(LFM)将无界重要性比率映射到有界可微域，防止高方差异常标记破坏损失景观；2) 解耦危险惩罚(DHP)整合生存分析中的累积危险函数，独立调节正负策略偏移，通过危险感知惩罚塑造优化景观。

Result: 在多种推理基准测试（文本和视觉语言任务）上的广泛评估表明，MHPO始终优于现有方法，在显著增强训练稳定性的同时实现了卓越性能。

Conclusion: MHPO通过梯度保真度保持和危险感知调节，实现了对不对称策略偏移的细粒度调控，同时缓解了过度扩展导致的模式崩溃和灾难性收缩导致的策略侵蚀，在稳定信任区域内实现了鲁棒稳定的强化学习。

Abstract: Regulating the importance ratio is critical for the training stability of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) based frameworks. However, prevailing ratio control methods, such as hard clipping, suffer from non-differentiable boundaries and vanishing gradient regions, failing to maintain gradient fidelity. Furthermore, these methods lack a hazard-aware mechanism to adaptively suppress extreme deviations, leaving the optimization process vulnerable to abrupt policy shifts. To address these challenges, we propose Modulated Hazard-aware Policy Optimization (MHPO), a novel framework designed for robust and stable reinforcement learning. The proposed MHPO introduces a Log-Fidelity Modulator (LFM) to map unbounded importance ratios into a bounded, differentiable domain. This mechanism effectively prevents high-variance outlier tokens from destabilizing the loss landscape while ensuring global gradient stability. Complementarily, a Decoupled Hazard Penalty (DHP) integrates cumulative hazard functions from survival analysis to independently regulate positive and negative policy shifts. By shaping the optimization landscape with hazard-aware penalties, the proposed MHPO achieves fine-grained regulation of asymmetric policy shifts simultaneously mitigating mode collapse from over-expansion and preventing policy erosion from catastrophic contraction within a stabilized trust region. Extensive evaluations on diverse reasoning benchmarks across both text-based and vision-language tasks demonstrate that MHPO consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving superior performance while significantly enhancing training stability.

</details>


### [71] [Integrating Explainable Machine Learning and Mixed-Integer Optimization for Personalized Sleep Quality Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16937)
*Mahfuz Ahmed Anik,Mohsin Mahmud Topu,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Md Isfar Khan,MD Manjurul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个个性化预测-处方框架，将可解释机器学习与混合整数优化结合，将睡眠质量预测转化为可操作的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算研究主要关注预测风险识别而非可操作的干预设计，机器学习模型能准确预测睡眠结果但很少转化为实际干预策略，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 集成可解释机器学习与混合整数优化：使用监督分类器预测睡眠质量，SHAP特征归因量化可修改因素的影响，将这些重要性度量纳入混合整数优化模型，识别最小且可行的行为调整，通过惩罚机制建模改变阻力。

Result: 框架取得强预测性能：测试F1分数0.9544，准确率0.9366。敏感性和帕累托分析显示预期改善与干预强度之间的权衡，随着额外变化引入出现收益递减。个体层面生成简洁建议，通常建议1-2个高影响力行为调整，当预期收益最小时有时建议不改变。

Conclusion: 通过集成预测、解释和约束优化，该框架展示了如何将数据驱动的洞察转化为结构化、个性化的睡眠改善决策支持，弥合预测分析与实际干预之间的差距。

Abstract: Sleep quality is influenced by a complex interplay of behavioral, environmental, and psychosocial factors, yet most computational studies focus mainly on predictive risk identification rather than actionable intervention design. Although machine learning models can accurately predict subjective sleep outcomes, they rarely translate predictive insights into practical intervention strategies. To address this gap, we propose a personalized predictive-prescriptive framework that integrates interpretable machine learning with mixed-integer optimization. A supervised classifier trained on survey data predicts sleep quality, while SHAP-based feature attribution quantifies the influence of modifiable factors. These importance measures are incorporated into a mixed-integer optimization model that identifies minimal and feasible behavioral adjustments, while modelling resistance to change through a penalty mechanism. The framework achieves strong predictive performance, with a test F1-score of 0.9544 and an accuracy of 0.9366. Sensitivity and Pareto analyses reveal a clear trade-off between expected improvement and intervention intensity, with diminishing returns as additional changes are introduced. At the individual level, the model generates concise recommendations, often suggesting one or two high-impact behavioral adjustments and sometimes recommending no change when expected gains are minimal. By integrating prediction, explanation, and constrained optimization, this framework demonstrates how data-driven insights can be translated into structured and personalized decision support for sleep improvement.

</details>


### [72] [Integrating Inductive Biases in Transformers via Distillation for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16985)
*Yu-Chen Den,Kuan-Yu Chen,Kendro Vincent,Darby Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIPS：通过知识蒸馏将因果性、局部性和周期性等不同归纳偏置整合到统一Transformer中，以应对金融时间序列的非平稳性和机制转换问题，在多个股票市场取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的时间序列预测模型通常隐含平稳性和稳定时间动态的假设，这与金融市场的机制转换和非平稳性相矛盾。实证表明，SOTA时间序列Transformer在金融任务上甚至不如普通Transformer，而具有特定归纳偏置的CNN和RNN等简单架构能以更低复杂度获得更好性能。同时，没有单一归纳偏置能在所有市场或机制中占主导，因此需要整合互补的时间先验知识以实现稳健的金融预测。

Method: 提出TIPS（Transformer with Inductive Prior Synthesis）知识蒸馏框架：1）通过注意力掩码训练具有特定归纳偏置（因果性、局部性、周期性）的Transformer教师模型；2）将教师模型的知识蒸馏到单一学生模型中，并在不同机制下进行跨归纳偏置的对齐；3）实现机制依赖的归纳偏置利用。

Result: 在四个主要股票市场中，TIPS实现了SOTA性能：年化收益率提升55%，夏普比率提升9%，卡尔玛比率提升16%，同时推理计算量仅需38%。进一步分析表明，TIPS能产生统计显著的超额收益，超越普通Transformer和教师模型集成，并在盈利期间与经典架构表现出机制依赖的行为对齐。

Conclusion: TIPS通过知识蒸馏整合多种归纳偏置，有效应对金融时间序列的非平稳性和机制转换问题。结果表明，机制依赖的归纳偏置利用对于非平稳金融时间序列的稳健泛化至关重要，为金融预测提供了新的架构设计思路。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have been widely adopted for time-series forecasting due to their high representational capacity and architectural flexibility. However, many Transformer variants implicitly assume stationarity and stable temporal dynamics -- assumptions routinely violated in financial markets characterized by regime shifts and non-stationarity. Empirically, state-of-the-art time-series Transformers often underperform even vanilla Transformers on financial tasks, while simpler architectures with distinct inductive biases, such as CNNs and RNNs, can achieve stronger performance with substantially lower complexity. At the same time, no single inductive bias dominates across markets or regimes, suggesting that robust financial forecasting requires integrating complementary temporal priors. We propose TIPS (Transformer with Inductive Prior Synthesis), a knowledge distillation framework that synthesizes diverse inductive biases -- causality, locality, and periodicity -- within a unified Transformer. TIPS trains bias-specialized Transformer teachers via attention masking, then distills their knowledge into a single student model with regime-dependent alignment across inductive biases. Across four major equity markets, TIPS achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming strong ensemble baselines by 55%, 9%, and 16% in annual return, Sharpe ratio, and Calmar ratio, while requiring only 38% of the inference-time computation. Further analyses show that TIPS generates statistically significant excess returns beyond both vanilla Transformers and its teacher ensembles, and exhibits regime-dependent behavioral alignment with classical architectures during their profitable periods. These results highlight the importance of regime-dependent inductive bias utilization for robust generalization in non-stationary financial time series.

</details>


### [73] [Transformers Can Learn Rules They've Never Seen: Proof of Computation Beyond Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17019)
*Andy Gray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer模型能够学习训练数据中未直接观察到的规则结构，而非仅进行基于相似性的插值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Transformer是否能够推断训练数据中未出现的规则，还是仅仅进行基于相似性的插值，以解决LLM泛化能力的本质问题

Method: 设计两个受控实验：1）使用纯XOR规则的细胞自动机，移除特定局部输入模式，通过电路提取分析计算机制；2）研究符号运算符链，要求模型输出中间步骤和最终答案的证明格式

Result: 实验1中两层Transformer恢复规则（最佳100%；47/60收敛运行），电路提取识别XOR计算；实验2中Transformer在所有49个保留对上超越所有插值基线（平均41.8%，最高78.6%），移除中间步骤监督会降低性能

Conclusion: Transformer能够学习训练中未直接观察到的规则结构并明确表达，排除了最强形式的"仅插值"架构假设，但大型语言训练中何时出现这种行为仍待研究

Abstract: A central question in the LLM debate is whether transformers can infer rules absent from training, or whether apparent generalisation reduces to similarity-based interpolation over observed examples. We test a strong interpolation-only hypothesis in two controlled settings: one where interpolation is ruled out by construction and proof, and one where success requires emitting intermediate symbolic derivations rather than only final answers. In Experiment 1, we use a cellular automaton with a pure XOR transition rule and remove specific local input patterns from training; since XOR is linearly inseparable, each held-out pattern's nearest neighbours have the opposite label, so similarity-based predictors fail on the held-out region. Yet a two-layer transformer recovers the rule (best 100%; 47/60 converged runs), and circuit extraction identifies XOR computation. Performance depends on multi-step constraint propagation: without unrolling, accuracy matches output bias (63.1%), while soft unrolling reaches 96.7%. In Experiment 2, we study symbolic operator chains over integers with one operator pair held out; the model must emit intermediate steps and a final answer in a proof-like format. Across all 49 holdout pairs, the transformer exceeds every interpolation baseline (mean 41.8%, up to 78.6%; mean KRR 4.3%; KNN and MLP score 0% on every pair), while removing intermediate-step supervision degrades performance. Together with a construction showing that a standard transformer block can implement exact local Boolean rules, these results provide an existence proof that transformers can learn rule structure not directly observed in training and express it explicitly, ruling out the strongest architectural form of interpolation-only accounts: that transformers cannot in principle discover and communicate unseen rules, while leaving open when such behaviour arises in large-scale language training.

</details>


### [74] [Do Understanding and Generation Fight? A Diagnostic Study of DPO for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17044)
*Abinav Rao,Sujan Rachuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPO无法同时对齐统一多模态模型的理解和生成能力，梯度正交性和幅度不平衡导致生成质量无法通过DPO对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DPO是否能同时对齐统一多模态模型（使用语言模型主干进行图像理解和生成）的理解和生成能力

Method: 在Janus-Pro 1B和7B参数模型上应用DPO，采用七种训练策略和两种后处理方法，分析梯度正交性和幅度不平衡

Result: 生成质量在所有测试条件下都无法通过DPO对齐：7B模型生成CLIPScore无改善，1B模型生成质量下降；理解和生成梯度接近正交（cos~0），幅度不平衡11-14倍，由VQ令牌数量不对称导致

Conclusion: 离散VQ令牌化是结构瓶颈，梯度不平衡是多任务DPO的主要干扰机制，为基于VQ的统一模型实践者提供指导

Abstract: Unified multimodal models share a language model backbone for both understanding and generating images. Can DPO align both capabilities simultaneously? We present the first systematic study of this question, applying DPO to Janus-Pro at 1B and 7B parameters under seven training strategies and two post-hoc methods. The central finding is negative: generation quality resists DPO alignment across all tested conditions on this architecture. No method improves generation CLIPScore at 7B (|Delta| < 0.2, p > 0.5 at n=200 per seed, 3 seeds); at 1B, all methods degrade generation, and the result holds across preference data types (real-vs-generated and model-vs-model) and the data volumes tested (150-288 pairs). Gradient analysis reveals why: understanding and generation gradients are near-orthogonal (cos ~ 0) with ~11-14x magnitude imbalance driven by VQ token count asymmetry (576 generation tokens vs. ~30-100 text tokens). This imbalance is the dominant interference mechanism in multi-task DPO; magnitude-balancing yields directionally positive understanding deltas (+0.01-0.04 VQA, though individually not significant), but the generation gap persists regardless. We identify discrete VQ tokenization as a likely structural bottleneck -- supported by the generation DPO loss converging to ln(2) -- and provide practical guidance for practitioners working with VQ-based unified models.

</details>


### [75] [Early Quantization Shrinks Codebook: A Simple Fix for Diversity-Preserving Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17052)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Rushi Shah,Yudi Wu,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了向量量化在生成模型中的表示坍缩问题，识别了两种坍缩类型及其触发条件，并提出了缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化在大型语言模型、扩散模型等生成模型中广泛用于数据表示的离散化，但其在生成模型中的特性和行为尚未得到充分探索，特别是表示坍缩问题缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 使用合成和真实数据集，系统研究向量量化中的坍缩问题，识别坍缩类型（离散码本标记坍缩和连续潜在嵌入坍缩）及其严重程度和触发条件，基于分析结果提出缓解方案。

Result: 研究发现随机初始化和有限编码器容量会导致标记坍缩和嵌入坍缩，揭示了每种坍缩类型的严重程度和触发条件，为理解向量量化中的表示坍缩问题提供了系统分析。

Conclusion: 这是首个全面研究向量量化中表示坍缩问题的工作，系统识别了坍缩类型和触发条件，提出的缓解方案有助于改进向量量化在生成模型中的应用。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a technique in machine learning that discretizes continuous representations into a set of discrete vectors. It is widely employed in tokenizing data representations for large language models, diffusion models, and other generative models. Despite its prevalence, the characteristics and behaviors of vector quantization in generative models remain largely underexplored. In this study, we systematically investigate the issue of collapses in vector quantization, where collapsed representations are observed across discrete codebook tokens and continuous latent embeddings. By leveraging both synthetic and real datasets, we identify the severity of each type of collapses and triggering conditions. Our analysis reveals that random initialization and limited encoder capacity result in tokens collapse and embeddings collapse. Building on these findings, we propose potential solutions aimed at mitigating each collapse. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive study examining representation collapsing problems in vector quantization.

</details>


### [76] [CircuitBuilder: From Polynomials to Circuits via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17075)
*Weikun K. Zhang,Rohan Pandey,Bhaumik Mehta,Kaijie Jin,Naomi Morato,Archit Ganapule,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Jarod Alper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用强化学习自动发现高效算术电路以计算多项式，将问题建模为单智能体游戏，比较PPO+MCTS和SAC两种方法在多项式电路合成任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受自动证明生成和Valiant的VP vs. VNP猜想启发，研究如何自动发现使用加法和乘法门计算多项式的高效算术电路，为研究自改进搜索策略提供紧凑可验证的环境。

Method: 将多项式电路合成问题建模为单智能体强化学习游戏，智能体在固定操作次数内构建电路。采用AlphaZero风格训练循环，比较两种方法：PPO与蒙特卡洛树搜索结合（PPO+MCTS）和软演员-评论家（SAC）。

Result: SAC在双变量目标上获得最高成功率，而PPO+MCTS能够扩展到三变量问题，并在更困难实例上表现出稳定改进。两种方法在不同复杂度问题上各有优势。

Conclusion: 多项式电路合成为研究自改进搜索策略提供了紧凑且可验证的实验环境，强化学习方法在该问题上展现出潜力，不同算法在不同复杂度问题上表现各异。

Abstract: Motivated by auto-proof generation and Valiant's VP vs. VNP conjecture, we study the problem of discovering efficient arithmetic circuits to compute polynomials, using addition and multiplication gates. We formulate this problem as a single-player game, where an RL agent attempts to build the circuit within a fixed number of operations. We implement an AlphaZero-style training loop and compare two approaches: Proximal Policy Optimization with Monte Carlo Tree Search (PPO+MCTS) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). SAC achieves the highest success rates on two-variable targets, while PPO+MCTS scales to three variables and demonstrates steady improvement on harder instances. These results suggest that polynomial circuit synthesis is a compact, verifiable setting for studying self-improving search policies.

</details>


### [77] [SENSE: Efficient EEG-to-Text via Privacy-Preserving Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17109)
*Akshaj Murhekar,Christina Liu,Abhijit Mishra,Shounak Roychowdhury,Jacek Gwizdka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SENSE是一个轻量级、保护隐私的脑电信号解码框架，通过两阶段方法将EEG信号转换为文本，无需微调大语言模型，仅需约600万参数即可在设备端运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑机接口方法通常需要微调大语言模型或编码器-解码器模型，导致训练成本高、可访问性有限，且可能暴露敏感的神经数据。需要一种轻量级、保护隐私的解决方案。

Method: SENSE采用两阶段解码：1) 设备端语义检索，将EEG信号本地映射到离散文本空间提取非敏感的词袋表示；2) 基于提示的语言生成，使用现成LLM以零样本方式合成流畅文本。EEG到关键词模块仅包含约600万参数，完全在设备端运行。

Result: 在6名受试者的128通道EEG数据集上评估，SENSE在生成质量上匹配或超越了完全微调的基线方法（如Thought2Text），同时显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: SENSE通过本地化神经解码并仅共享派生的文本线索，为下一代脑机接口提供了一个可扩展且具有隐私意识的检索增强架构。

Abstract: Decoding brain activity into natural language is a major challenge in AI with important applications in assistive communication, neurotechnology, and human-computer interaction. Most existing Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) approaches rely on memory-intensive fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs) or encoder-decoder models on raw EEG signals, resulting in expensive training pipelines, limited accessibility, and potential exposure of sensitive neural data. We introduce SENSE (SEmantic Neural Sparse Extraction), a lightweight and privacy-preserving framework that translates non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG) into text without LLM fine-tuning. SENSE decouples decoding into two stages: on-device semantic retrieval and prompt-based language generation. EEG signals are locally mapped to a discrete textual space to extract a non-sensitive Bag-of-Words (BoW), which conditions an off-the-shelf LLM to synthesize fluent text in a zero-shot manner. The EEG-to-keyword module contains only ~6M parameters and runs fully on-device, ensuring raw neural signals remain local while only abstract semantic cues interact with language models. Evaluated on a 128-channel EEG dataset across six subjects, SENSE matches or surpasses the generative quality of fully fine-tuned baselines such as Thought2Text while substantially reducing computational overhead. By localizing neural decoding and sharing only derived textual cues, SENSE provides a scalable and privacy-aware retrieval-augmented architecture for next-generation BCIs.

</details>


### [78] [Contextual Preference Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17139)
*Benjamin Hudson,Laurent Charlin,Emma Frejinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个序列学习-优化管道，通过有界方差得分函数梯度估计器学习偏好分布，用于解决具有人类偏好不确定性的决策问题，特别适用于风险规避场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策问题常面临来自异质性和上下文依赖的人类偏好的不确定性。现有逆优化和选择建模方法通常产生点估计或无法捕捉上下文变化，不适合风险规避决策。

Method: 使用有界方差得分函数梯度估计器训练预测模型，将上下文特征映射到可参数化的分布类，获得最大似然估计。模型为后续优化阶段生成未见上下文的场景。

Result: 在合成拼车环境中，该方法相比具有完美预测的风险中性方法将平均决策后意外降低114倍，相比领先的风险规避基线降低25倍。

Conclusion: 提出的序列学习-优化管道能有效学习偏好分布并用于下游风险规避决策，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Decision-making problems often feature uncertainty stemming from heterogeneous and context-dependent human preferences. To address this, we propose a sequential learning-and-optimization pipeline to learn preference distributions and leverage them to solve downstream problems, for example risk-averse formulations. We focus on human choice settings that can be formulated as (integer) linear programs. In such settings, existing inverse optimization and choice modelling methods infer preferences from observed choices but typically produce point estimates or fail to capture contextual shifts, making them unsuitable for risk-averse decision-making. Using a bounded-variance score function gradient estimator, we train a predictive model mapping contextual features to a rich class of parameterizable distributions. This approach yields a maximum likelihood estimate. The model generates scenarios for unseen contexts in the subsequent optimization phase. In a synthetic ridesharing environment, our approach reduces average post-decision surprise by up to 114$\times$ compared to a risk-neutral approach with perfect predictions and up to 25$\times$ compared to leading risk-averse baselines.

</details>


### [79] [REAL: Regression-Aware Reinforcement Learning for LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17145)
*Yasi Zhang,Tianyu Chen,Mingyuan Zhou,Oscar Leong,Ying Nian Wu,Michal Lukasik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REAL框架，将回归目标整合到强化学习中，解决LLM评估中回归任务的有序结构问题，相比传统RL方法和回归感知SFT基线有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为评估器（LLM-as-a-Judge）时存在两个问题：1）标准RL方法依赖二元奖励，忽略了回归任务固有的有序结构；2）现有回归感知方法局限于监督微调，无法探索最优推理路径。需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出REAL（回归感知强化学习）框架，采用广义策略梯度估计器，将优化分解为两个互补组件：1）在思维链轨迹上进行探索；2）对最终分数的回归感知预测细化。

Result: 在8B到32B不同规模模型上的实验表明，REAL始终优于回归感知SFT基线和标准RL方法，在域外基准测试中表现出更好的泛化能力。在Qwen3-32B上，相比SFT基线获得+8.40皮尔逊和+7.20斯皮尔曼相关性提升。

Conclusion: 研究强调了将回归目标整合到RL探索中对准确LLM评估的关键价值，REAL框架为回归感知的强化学习提供了原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as automated evaluators that assign numeric scores to model outputs, a paradigm known as LLM-as-a-Judge. However, standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods typically rely on binary rewards (e.g., 0-1 accuracy), thereby ignoring the ordinal structure inherent in regression tasks; for instance, they fail to recognize that predicting 4 is significantly better than predicting 1 when the ground truth is 5. Conversely, existing regression-aware approaches are often confined to Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), limiting their ability to explore optimal reasoning paths. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{REAL} (\underline{RE}gression-\underline{A}ware Reinforcement \underline{L}earning), a principled RL framework designed to optimize regression rewards, and also proven to be optimal for correlation metrics. A key technical challenge is that the regression objective is explicitly policy-dependent, thus invalidating standard policy gradient methods. To address this, we employ the generalized policy gradient estimator, which naturally decomposes optimization into two complementary components: (1) exploration over Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectory, and (2) regression-aware prediction refinement of the final score. Extensive experiments across model scales (8B to 32B) demonstrate that REAL consistently outperforms both regression-aware SFT baselines and standard RL methods, exhibiting significantly better generalization on out-of-domain benchmarks. On Qwen3-32B specifically, we achieve gains of +8.40 Pearson and +7.20 Spearman correlation over the SFT baseline, and +18.30/+11.20 over the base model. These findings highlight the critical value of integrating regression objectives into RL exploration for accurate LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [80] [Personalized Fall Detection by Balancing Data with Selective Feedback Using Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17148)
*Awatif Yasmin,Tarek Mahmud,Sana Alamgeer,Anne H. H. Ngu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合半监督聚类和对比学习的个性化跌倒检测框架，通过识别和平衡最有价值的用户反馈样本来解决数据不平衡问题，在三种重训练策略中TFS表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化跌倒检测模型受限于真实跌倒数据的稀缺性和非跌倒反馈样本的主导地位，这种数据不平衡导致模型偏向常规活动，降低了对真实跌倒事件的敏感性。

Method: 提出结合半监督聚类和对比学习的个性化框架，识别和平衡最有价值的用户反馈样本。评估三种重训练策略：从头训练(TFS)、迁移学习(TL)和少样本学习(FSL)，以评估不同学习范式的适应性。

Result: 10名参与者的实时实验显示，TFS方法性能最高，比基线提升25%；FSL次之，提升7%。证明选择性个性化在真实世界部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的个性化框架通过平衡信息性反馈样本有效解决了跌倒检测中的数据不平衡问题，TFS策略在个性化适应方面表现最佳，为真实世界部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized fall detection models can significantly improve accuracy by adapting to individual motion patterns, yet their effectiveness is often limited by the scarcity of real-world fall data and the dominance of non-fall feedback samples. This imbalance biases the model toward routine activities and weakens its sensitivity to true fall events. To address this challenge, we propose a personalization framework that combines semi-supervised clustering with contrastive learning to identify and balance the most informative user feedback samples. The framework is evaluated under three retraining strategies, including Training from Scratch (TFS), Transfer Learning (TL), and Few-Shot Learning (FSL), to assess adaptability across learning paradigms. Real-time experiments with ten participants show that the TFS approach achieves the highest performance, with up to a 25% improvement over the baseline, while FSL achieves the second-highest performance with a 7% improvement, demonstrating the effectiveness of selective personalization for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [81] [Benchmarking Reinforcement Learning via Stochastic Converse Optimality: Generating Systems with Known Optimal Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17631)
*Sinan Ibrahim,Grégoire Ouerdane,Hadi Salloum,Henni Ouerdane,Stefan Streif,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于逆最优性的强化学习基准测试框架，通过构造已知最优解的系统来精确评估算法性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法的客观比较极其复杂，因为结果对环境设计、奖励结构和随机性高度敏感，需要建立可重复、精确的基准测试方法

Method: 将逆最优性扩展到离散时间、控制仿射、带噪声的非线性系统，提供必要和充分条件来构造已知最优值函数和策略的系统，通过同伦变化和随机参数生成基准测试族

Result: 框架能够自动构建多样化环境，实现对算法的受控和全面评估，通过对比标准方法与真实最优解，为精确的RL基准测试提供可重复基础

Conclusion: 该框架为强化学习算法提供了严谨的基准测试方法，解决了性能评估中的复杂性问题，实现了对算法在已知最优解系统上的精确比较

Abstract: The objective comparison of Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms is notoriously complex as outcomes and benchmarking of performances of different RL approaches are critically sensitive to environmental design, reward structures, and stochasticity inherent in both algorithmic learning and environmental dynamics. To manage this complexity, we introduce a rigorous benchmarking framework by extending converse optimality to discrete-time, control-affine, nonlinear systems with noise. Our framework provides necessary and sufficient conditions, under which a prescribed value function and policy are optimal for constructed systems, enabling the systematic generation of benchmark families via homotopy variations and randomized parameters. We validate it by automatically constructing diverse environments, demonstrating our framework's capacity for a controlled and comprehensive evaluation across algorithms. By assessing standard methods against a ground-truth optimum, our work delivers a reproducible foundation for precise and rigorous RL benchmarking.

</details>


### [82] [Noise-Response Calibration: A Causal Intervention Protocol for LLM-Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17172)
*Maxim Khomiakov,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于控制输入干预的LLM校准协议：通过噪声干预测试性能下降趋势，发现文本与表格数据存在模态差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评估器和合成标注器使用时存在随机性和过度自信问题，在缺乏外部真实标签时难以做出部署决策

Method: 提出基于控制输入干预的校准协议：通过增加噪声严重程度，测试任务性能是否呈现统计显著的恶化趋势；使用基于斜率的假设检验，对表格数据采用信噪比扰动，对文本数据采用词汇扰动

Result: 发现明显的模态依赖行为：文本评估器可预测地退化，而大多数表格数据集即使在显著信噪比降低下也缺乏统计显著的性能恶化；有趣的是，对噪声干预不敏感的数据集上模型性能较低

Conclusion: 提出了可复现的方法论和报告协议，用于在分布偏移下进行稳健的LLM评估器校准，揭示了文本与表格数据之间的模态差异

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges and synthetic labelers, especially in low-label settings. Yet these systems are stochastic and often overconfident, which makes deployment decisions difficult when external ground truth is limited. We propose a practical calibration protocol based on controlled input interventions: if noise severity increases, task performance should exhibit a statistically significant deterioration trend. We operationalize this with a slope-based hypothesis test over repeated trials, using signal-to-noise-ratio (SNR) perturbations for tabular data and lexical perturbations for text data. Across UCI tabular benchmarks and four text classification datasets, we find clear modality-dependent behavior. Our results reveal a modality gap: while text-based judges degrade predictably, the majority of tabular datasets show a lack of statistically significant performance deterioration even under significant signal-to-noise reduction. Interestingly we find that model performance is lower on datasets that are insensitive to noise interventions.
